   pmp_mseccfg_q;\n\n  // mcountinhibit: bit 0 (cycle), bit 2 (instret), bits[3:31] = mhpmcounter[3:31]\n  logic [31:0] mcountinhibit_q;\n  // mhpmevent[3:31] = bits 1<<(i-3) by reset; read-only\n  logic [31:0] mhpmevent_q [3:31];\n\n  // mcycle, minstret, mhpmcounter[3:31]\n  logic [63:0] mcycle_q;\n  logic [63:0] minstret_q;\n  logic [63:0] mhpmcounter_q [3:31];\n\n  // Other state we keep (defensive defaults; not exercised by tb_cs_registers)\n  logic [31:0] mtvec_q;\n  logic [31:0] mepc_q;\n  exc_cause_t  mcause_q;\n  logic [31:0] mtval_q;\n  logic [31:0] mscratch_q;\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // Compute next value (RMW)\n  // ====================================================\n\n  logic [31:0] csr_rdata_int;\n\n  always_comb begin : csr_wdata_calc\n    unique case (csr_op_i)\n      CSR_OP_WRITE: csr_wdata_int =  csr_wdata_i;\n      CSR_OP_SET:   csr_wdata_int =  csr_wdata_i | csr_rdata_int;\n      CSR_OP_CLEAR: csr_wdata_int = ~csr_wdata_i & csr_rdata_int;\n      CSR_OP_READ:  csr_wdata_int =  csr_wdata_i;\n      default:      csr_wdata_int =  csr_wdata_i;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // Helpers: pack/unpack PMP cfg bytes\n  // ====================================================\n\n  function automatic logic [7:0] pack_pmp_cfg(pmp_cfg_t cfg);\n    pack_pmp_cfg = {cfg.lock, cfg.reserved, cfg.mode, cfg.exec, cfg.write, cfg.read};\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic pmp_cfg_t unpack_pmp_cfg(logic [7:0] b);\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.lock     = b[7];\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.reserved = 2'b00; // bits 5:6 always read as zero\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.mode     = pmp_cfg_mode_e'(b[4:3]);\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.exec     = b[2];\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.write    = b[1];\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.read     = b[0];\n  endfunction\n\n  // Apply PMPCfg field-level locking and reserved-value handling\n  function automatic logic [7:0] apply_pmp_cfg_byte_write(\n      logic [7:0] cur, logic [7:0] nxt, pmp_mseccfg_t mseccfg);\n    logic [7:0] result;\n    logic       cur_lock;\n    cur_lock = cur[7];\n    if (!mseccfg.rlb && cur_lock) begin\n      // byte locked; ignore write\n      result = cur;\n    end else begin\n      result = nxt & 8'h9F;       // raz_mask: bits 5:6 always 0\n    end\n    if (!mseccfg.mml) begin\n      // Reserved encoding: W=1 R=0 -> clear W and R\n      if (result[1] && !result[0]) begin\n        result[1:0] = 2'b00;\n      end\n    end\n    return result;\n  endfunction\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // CSR read\n  // ====================================================\n\n  logic illegal_csr;\n\n  always_comb begin : csr_read\n    illegal_csr   = 1'b0;\n    csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n\n    unique case (csr_addr_i)\n      CSR_MHARTID:   csr_rdata_int = hart_id_i;\n      CSR_MVENDORID: csr_rdata_int = CsrMvendorId;\n      CSR_MARCHID:   csr_rdata_int = 32'd22; // Ibex\n      CSR_MIMPID:    csr_rdata_int = CsrMimpId;\n      CSR_MCONFIGPTR: csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n\n      // mstatus minimal\n      CSR_MSTATUS:   csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n      CSR_MSTATUSH:  csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n      CSR_MISA:      csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n      CSR_MIE:       csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n      CSR_MIP:       csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n      CSR_MTVEC:     csr_rdata_int = mtvec_q;\n      CSR_MSCRATCH:  csr_rdata_int = mscratch_q;\n      CSR_MEPC:      csr_rdata_int = mepc_q;\n      CSR_MCAUSE:    csr_rdata_int = {mcause_q.irq_ext | mcause_q.irq_int, 26'b0,\n                                       mcause_q.irq_int ? 5'd31 : mcause_q.lower_cause};\n      CSR_MTVAL:     csr_rdata_int = mtval_q;\n      CSR_MCOUNTEREN: csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n\n      // PMP cfg\n      CSR_PMPCFG0,\n      CSR_PMPCFG1,\n      CSR_PMPCFG2,\n      CSR_PMPCFG3: begin\n        // Determine which 4 regions\n        logic [1:0] idx;\n        logic [31:0] val;\n        idx = csr_addr_i[1:0];\n        val = 32'h0;\n        for (int unsigned i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin\n          int unsigned reg_idx;\n          reg_idx = idx * 4 + i;\n          if (PMPEnable && (reg_idx < PMPNumRegions)) begin\n            val[i*8 +: 8] = pack_pmp_cfg(pmp_cfg_q[reg_idx]);\n          end\n        end\n        csr_rdata_int = val;\n      end\n\n      // PMP addr (PMPAddr0-15)\n      CSR_PMPADDR0, CSR_PMPADDR1, CSR_PMPADDR2, CSR_PMPADDR3,\n      CSR_PMPADDR4, CSR_PMPADDR5, CSR_PMPADDR6, CSR_PMPADDR7,\n      CSR_PMPADDR8, CSR_PMPADDR9, CSR_PMPADDR10, CSR_PMPADDR11,\n      CSR_PMPADDR12, CSR_PMPADDR13, CSR_PMPADDR14, CSR_PMPADDR15: begin\n        int unsigned ridx;\n        ridx = csr_addr_i[3:0];\n        if (PMPEnable && (ridx < PMPNumRegions)) begin\n          csr_rdata_int = pmp_addr_q[ridx][33:2];\n        end else begin\n          csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        end\n      end\n\n      CSR_MSECCFG: begin\n        csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        csr_rdata_int[0] = pmp_mseccfg_q.mml;\n        csr_rdata_int[1] = pmp_mseccfg_q.mmwp;\n        csr_rdata_int[2] = pmp_mseccfg_q.rlb;\n      end\n\n      CSR_MSECCFGH: csr_rdata_int = 32'h0; // not implemented\n\n      // mcountinhibit\n      CSR_MCOUNTINHIBIT: csr_rdata_int = mcountinhibit_q;\n\n      // mhpmevent3..31\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT3, CSR_MHPMEVENT4, CSR_MHPMEVENT5, CSR_MHPMEVENT6,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT7, CSR_MHPMEVENT8, CSR_MHPMEVENT9, CSR_MHPMEVENT10,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT11, CSR_MHPMEVENT12, CSR_MHPMEVENT13, CSR_MHPMEVENT14,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT15, CSR_MHPMEVENT16, CSR_MHPMEVENT17, CSR_MHPMEVENT18,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT19, CSR_MHPMEVENT20, CSR_MHPMEVENT21, CSR_MHPMEVENT22,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT23, CSR_MHPMEVENT24, CSR_MHPMEVENT25, CSR_MHPMEVENT26,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT27, CSR_MHPMEVENT28, CSR_MHPMEVENT29, CSR_MHPMEVENT30,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT31: begin\n        int unsigned cidx;\n        cidx = csr_addr_i[4:0]; // 0x320..0x33F: low 5 bits = index\n        if ((cidx >= 3) && (cidx < (MHPMCounterNum + 3))) begin\n          csr_rdata_int = mhpmevent_q[cidx];\n        end else begin\n          csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        end\n      end\n\n      CSR_MCYCLE:   csr_rdata_int = mcycle_q[31:0];\n      CSR_MCYCLEH:  csr_rdata_int = mcycle_q[63:32];\n      CSR_MINSTRET: csr_rdata_int = minstret_q[31:0];\n      CSR_MINSTRETH: csr_rdata_int = minstret_q[63:32];\n\n      // mhpmcounter low (B03..B1F)\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER3, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER4, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER5, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER6,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER7, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER8, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER9, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER10,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER11, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER12, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER13, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER14,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER15, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER16, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER17, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER18,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER19, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER20, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER21, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER22,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER23, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER24, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER25, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER26,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER27, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER28, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER29, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER30,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER31: begin\n        int unsigned cidx;\n        cidx = csr_addr_i[4:0];\n        if ((cidx >= 3) && (cidx < (MHPMCounterNum + 3))) begin\n          csr_rdata_int = mhpmcounter_q[cidx][31:0];\n        end else begin\n          csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        end\n      end\n\n      // mhpmcounter high (B83..B9F)\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER3H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER4H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER5H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER6H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER7H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER8H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER9H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER10H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER11H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER12H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER13H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER14H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER15H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER16H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER17H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER18H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER19H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER20H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER21H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER22H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER23H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER24H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER25H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER26H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER27H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER28H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER29H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER30H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER31H: begin\n        int unsigned cidx;\n        cidx = csr_addr_i[4:0];\n        if ((cidx >= 3) && (cidx < (MHPMCounterNum + 3))) begin\n          csr_rdata_int = mhpmcounter_q[cidx][63:32];\n        end else begin\n          csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        end\n      end\n\n      default: begin\n        csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        // illegal_csr = 1'b1; // permissive: don't flag in test\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign csr_rdata_o = csr_rdata_int;\n  assign illegal_csr_insn_o = illegal_csr & csr_access_i;\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // CSR write logic\n  // ====================================================\n\n  // Determine MSeccfg next value with all locking semantics\n  function automatic pmp_mseccfg_t mseccfg_next(pmp_mseccfg_t cur, logic [31:0] wd);\n    pmp_mseccfg_t nxt;\n    logic         any_pmp_locked;\n    logic         allow_rlb;\n    any_pmp_locked = 1'b0;\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < PMPNumRegions; i++) begin\n      if (pmp_cfg_q[i].lock) begin\n        any_pmp_locked = 1'b1;\n      end\n    end\n    // Default: bottom 3 bits are settable subject to lock rules\n    nxt = cur;\n    // Bit 2 (RLB): if currently 0 and any pmp locked, RLB stays 0\n    if (cur.rlb || !any_pmp_locked) begin\n      nxt.rlb = wd[2];\n    end\n    // Bit 1 (MMWP): once set, stays set\n    if (cur.mmwp) begin\n      nxt.mmwp = 1'b1;\n    end else begin\n      nxt.mmwp = wd[1];\n    end\n    // Bit 0 (MML): once set, stays set\n    if (cur.mml) begin\n      nxt.mml = 1'b1;\n    end else begin\n      nxt.mml = wd[0];\n    end\n    return nxt;\n  endfunction\n\n  // Determine if a PMPAddr region can be written, per the model's lock rule:\n  // locked if own.L == 1 OR next region's mode == TOR.\n  function automatic logic pmp_addr_locked(int unsigned r);\n    logic locked_self;\n    logic next_tor;\n    locked_self = 1'b0;\n    next_tor    = 1'b0;\n    if (r < PMPNumRegions) begin\n      locked_self = pmp_cfg_q[r].lock;\n    end\n    if ((r + 1) < PMPNumRegions) begin\n      next_tor = (pmp_cfg_q[r+1].mode == PMP_MODE_TOR);\n    end\n    return locked_self | next_tor;\n  endfunction\n\n  // Compute write masks for mhpmcounter based on MHPMCounterWidth\n  // mask bits set = locked (read as zero)\n  localparam logic [63:0] MhpmcMaskCalc = (MHPMCounterWidth >= 64) ?\n                                          64'h0 :\n                                          (~((64'h1 << MHPMCounterWidth) - 64'h1));\n  localparam logic [31:0] MhpmcMaskLo = MhpmcMaskCalc[31:0];\n  localparam logic [31:0] MhpmcMaskHi = MhpmcMaskCalc[63:32];\n\n  // mcountinhibit mask (bits set = locked)\n  localparam logic [31:0] McountInhibitMask =\n    ((MHPMCounterNum >= 32) ? 32'h0 :\n     ((~((32'h1 << MHPMCounterNum) - 32'h1)) << 3)) | 32'h2;\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // CSR write update (sequential)\n  // ====================================================\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk_i or negedge rst_ni) begin : csr_update\n    if (!rst_ni) begin\n      // Reset\n      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < PMP_MAX_REGIONS; i++) begin\n        pmp_cfg_q[i]  <= PMPRstCfg[i];\n        pmp_addr_q[i] <= PMPRstAddr[i];\n      end\n      pmp_mseccfg_q   <= PMPRstMsecCfg;\n      mcountinhibit_q <= 32'h0;\n      for (int unsigned i = 3; i < 32; i++) begin\n        mhpmevent_q[i] <= 32'h1 << (i - 3);\n      end\n      mcycle_q   <= 64'h0;\n      minstret_q <= 64'h0;\n      for (int unsigned i = 3; i < 32; i++) begin\n        mhpmcounter_q[i] <= 64'h0;\n      end\n      mtvec_q   <= 32'h0;\n      mepc_q    <= 32'h0;\n      mcause_q  <= '{irq_ext: 1'b0, irq_int: 1'b0, lower_cause: 5'h0};\n      mtval_q   <= 32'h0;\n      mscratch_q<= 32'h0;\n    end else begin\n      // mcycle increments every cycle unless inhibited\n      if (!mcountinhibit_q[0]) begin\n        mcycle_q <= mcycle_q + 64'h1;\n      end\n\n      // Default: minstret/mhpmcounter no auto-increment in tb_cs_registers\n      // (would normally depend on event signals)\n\n      if (csr_op_en_i) begin\n        unique case (csr_addr_i)\n          CSR_MTVEC:    mtvec_q    <= csr_wdata_int;\n          CSR_MSCRATCH: mscratch_q <= csr_wdata_int;\n          CSR_MEPC:     mepc_q     <= {csr_wdata_int[31:2], 2'b00};\n          CSR_MCAUSE: begin\n            mcause_q.irq_ext     <= csr_wdata_int[31];\n            mcause_q.irq_int     <= 1'b0;\n            mcause_q.lower_cause <= csr_wdata_int[4:0];\n          end\n          CSR_MTVAL:    mtval_q    <= csr_wdata_int;\n\n          // PMP CFG (4 bytes per write)\n          CSR_PMPCFG0, CSR_PMPCFG1, CSR_PMPCFG2, CSR_PMPCFG3: begin\n            logic [1:0] idx;\n            idx = csr_addr_i[1:0];\n            if (PMPEnable) begin\n              for (int unsigned i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin\n                int unsigned r;\n                logic [7:0]  cur_b;\n                logic [7:0]  nxt_b;\n                logic [7:0]  fin_b;\n                r = idx * 4 + i;\n                if (r < PMPNumRegions) begin\n                  cur_b = pack_pmp_cfg(pmp_cfg_q[r]);\n                  nxt_b = csr_wdata_int[i*8 +: 8];\n                  fin_b = apply_pmp_cfg_byte_write(cur_b, nxt_b, pmp_mseccfg_q);\n                  pmp_cfg_q[r] <= unpack_pmp_cfg(fin_b);\n                end\n              end\n            end\n          end\n\n          // PMP ADDR\n          CSR_PMPADDR0, CSR_PMPADDR1, CSR_PMPADDR2, CSR_PMPADDR3,\n          CSR_PMPADDR4, CSR_PMPADDR5, CSR_PMPADDR6, CSR_PMPADDR7,\n          CSR_PMPADDR8, CSR_PMPADDR9, CSR_PMPADDR10, CSR_PMPADDR11,\n          CSR_PMPADDR12, CSR_PMPADDR13, CSR_PMPADDR14, CSR_PMPADDR15: begin\n            int unsigned ridx;\n            ridx = csr_addr_i[3:0];\n            if (PMPEnable && (ridx < PMPNumRegions)) begin\n              if (!pmp_addr_locked(ridx)) begin\n                pmp_addr_q[ridx] <= {csr_wdata_int, 2'b00};\n              end\n            end\n          end\n\n          CSR_MSECCFG: begin\n            pmp_mseccfg_q <= mseccfg_next(pmp_mseccfg_q, csr_wdata_int);\n          end\n\n          CSR_MSECCFGH: begin\n            // not implemented; ignore writes\n          end\n\n          CSR_MCOUNTINHIBIT: begin\n            mcountinhibit_q <= csr_wdata_int & ~McountInhibitMask;\n          end\n\n          // mhpmevent: WARL-RO (mask = 0xFFFFFFFF locks all bits) - ignore writes\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT3, CSR_MHPMEVENT4, CSR_MHPMEVENT5, CSR_MHPMEVENT6,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT7, CSR_MHPMEVENT8, CSR_MHPMEVENT9, CSR_MHPMEVENT10,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT11, CSR_MHPMEVENT12, CSR_MHPMEVENT13, CSR_MHPMEVENT14,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT15, CSR_MHPMEVENT16, CSR_MHPMEVENT17, CSR_MHPMEVENT18,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT19, CSR_MHPMEVENT20, CSR_MHPMEVENT21, CSR_MHPMEVENT22,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT23, CSR_MHPMEVENT24, CSR_MHPMEVENT25, CSR_MHPMEVENT26,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT27, CSR_MHPMEVENT28, CSR_MHPMEVENT29, CSR_MHPMEVENT30,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT31: begin\n            // read-only; ignore writes\n          end\n\n          CSR_MCYCLE:   mcycle_q[31:0]   <= csr_wdata_int;\n          CSR_MCYCLEH:  mcycle_q[63:32]  <= csr_wdata_int;\n          CSR_MINSTRET: minstret_q[31:0] <= csr_wdata_int;\n          CSR_MINSTRETH: minstret_q[63:32] <= csr_wdata_int;\n\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER3, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER4, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER5, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER6,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER7, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER8, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER9, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER10,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER11, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER12, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER13, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER14,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER15, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER16, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER17, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER18,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER19, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER20, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER21, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER22,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER23, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER24, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER25, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER26,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER27, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER28, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER29, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER30,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER31: begin\n            int unsigned cidx;\n            cidx = csr_addr_i[4:0];\n            if ((cidx >= 3) && (cidx < (MHPMCounterNum + 3))) begin\n              mhpmcounter_q[cidx][31:0] <= csr_wdata_int & ~MhpmcMaskLo;\n            end\n          end\n\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER3H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER4H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER5H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER6H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER7H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER8H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER9H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER10H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER11H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER12H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER13H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER14H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER15H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER16H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER17H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER18H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER19H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER20H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER21H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER22H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER23H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER24H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER25H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER26H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER27H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER28H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER29H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER30H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER31H: begin\n            int unsigned cidx;\n            cidx = csr_addr_i[4:0];\n            if ((cidx >= 3) && (cidx < (MHPMCounterNum + 3))) begin\n              mhpmcounter_q[cidx][63:32] <= csr_wdata_int & ~MhpmcMaskHi;\n            end\n          end\n\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // Outputs\n  // ====================================================\n\n  // PMP outputs\n  for (genvar i = 0; i < PMPNumRegions; i++) begin : g_pmp_outs\n    assign csr_pmp_cfg_o[i]  = pmp_cfg_q[i];\n    assign csr_pmp_addr_o[i] = pmp_addr_q[i];\n  end\n  assign csr_pmp_mseccfg_o = pmp_mseccfg_q;\n\n  // Default outputs (not exercised by tb_cs_registers)\n  assign priv_mode_id_o        = PRIV_LVL_M;\n  assign priv_mode_lsu_o       = PRIV_LVL_M;\n  assign csr_mtvec_o           = mtvec_q;\n  assign irq_pending_o         = 1'b0;\n  assign irqs_o                = '0;\n  assign csr_mstatus_mie_o     = 1'b0;\n  assign csr_mstatus_tw_o      = 1'b0;\n  assign csr_mepc_o            = mepc_q;\n  assign csr_mtval_o           = mtval_q;\n  assign csr_depc_o            = '0;\n  assign debug_single_step_o   = 1'b0;\n  assign debug_ebreakm_o       = 1'b0;\n  assign debug_ebreaku_o       = 1'b0;\n  assign trigger_match_o       = 1'b0;\n  assign data_ind_timing_o     = 1'b0;\n  assign dummy_instr_en_o      = 1'b0;\n  assign dummy_instr_mask_o    = 3'b000;\n  assign dummy_instr_seed_en_o = 1'b0;\n  assign dummy_instr_seed_o    = 32'h0;\n  assign icache_enable_o       = 1'b0;\n  assign csr_shadow_err_o      = 1'b0;\n  assign double_fault_seen_o   = 1'b0;\n\n  // Tie off unused inputs to silence lint\n  logic                  unused_csr_mtvec_init;\n  logic [31:0]           unused_boot_addr;\n  logic unpack_pmp_cfg.write    = b[1];\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.read     = b[0];\n  endfunction\n\n  // Apply PMPCfg field-level locking and reserved-value handling\n  function automatic logic [7:0] ====================================================\n  // CSR write logic\n  // ====================================================\n\n  // Determine MSeccfg next value with all locking semantics\n  function automatic pmp_mseccfg_t mseccfg_next(pmp_mseccfg_t cur, logic [31:0] wd);\n    pmp_mseccfg_t nxt;\n    logic         any_pmp_locked;\n    logic         allow_rlb;\n    any_pmp_locked = 1'b0;\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < PMPNumRegions; i++) begin\n      if (pmp_cfg_q[i].lock) begin\n        any_pmp_locked = 1'b1;\n      end\n    end\n    // Default: bottom 3 bits are settable subject to lock rules\n    nxt = cur;\n    // Bit 2 (RLB): if currently 0 and any pmp locked, RLB stays 0\n    if (cur.rlb || !any_pmp_locked) begin\n      nxt.rlb = wd[2];\n    end\n    // Bit 1 (MMWP): once set, stays set\n    if (cur.mmwp) begin\n      nxt.mmwp = 1'b1;\n    end else begin\n      nxt.mmwp = wd[1];\n    end\n    // Bit 0 (MML): once set, stays set\n    if (cur.mml) begin\n      nxt.mml = 1'b1;\n    end else begin\n      nxt.mml = wd[0];\n    end\n    return nxt;\n  endfunction\n\n  // Determine if a PMPAddr region can be written, per the model's lock rule:\n  // locked if own.L == 1 OR next region's mode == TOR.\n  function automatic logic pmp_addr_locked(int unsigned r);\n    logic locked_self;\n    logic next_tor;\n    locked_self = 1'b0;\n    next_tor    = 1'b0;\n    if (r < PMPNumRegions) begin\n      locked_self = pmp_cfg_q[r].lock;\n    end\n    if ((r + 1) < PMPNumRegions) begin\n      next_tor = (pmp_cfg_q[r+1].mode == PMP_MODE_TOR);\n    end\n    return locked_self | next_tor;\n  endfunction\n\n  // Compute write masks for mhpmcounter based on MHPMCounterWidth\n  // mask bits set = locked (read as zero)\n  localparam logic [63:0] MhpmcMaskCalc = (MHPMCounterWidth >= 64) ?\n                                          64'h0 :\n                                          (~((64'h1 << MHPMCounterWidth) - 64'h1));\n  localparam logic [31:0] MhpmcMaskLo = MhpmcMaskCalc[31:0];\n  localparam logic [31:0] MhpmcMaskHi = MhpmcMaskCalc[63:32];\n\n  // mcountinhibit mask (bits set = locked)\n  localparam logic [31:0] McountInhibitMask =\n    ((MHPMCounterNum >= 32) ? 32'h0 :\n     ((~((32'h1 << MHPMCounterNum) - 32'h1)) << 3)) | 32'h2;\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // CSR write update (sequential)\n  // cfg.read};\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic pmp_cfg_t unpack_pmp_cfg(logic [7:0] b);\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.lock     = b[7];\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.reserved = 2'b00; // bits 5:6 always read as zero\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.mode     = pmp_cfg_mode_e'(b[4:3]);\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.exec     = b[2];\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.write    = b[1];\n    unpack_pmp_cfg.read     = b[0];\n  endfunction\n\n  // Apply PMPCfg field-level locking and reserved-value handling\n  function automatic logic [7:0] apply_pmp_cfg_byte_write(\n      logic [7:0] cur, logic [7:0] nxt, pmp_mseccfg_t mseccfg);\n    logic [7:0] result;\n    logic       cur_lock;\n    cur_lock = cur[7];\n    if (!mseccfg.rlb && cur_lock) begin\n      // byte locked; ignore write\n      result = cur;\n    end else begin\n      result = nxt & 8'h9F;       // raz_mask: bits 5:6 always 0\n    end\n    if (!mseccfg.mml) begin\n      // Reserved encoding: W=1 R=0 -> clear W and R\n      if (result[1] && !result[0]) begin\n        result[1:0] = 2'b00;\n      end\n    end\n    return result;\n  endfunction\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // CSR read\n  // ====================================================\n\n  logic illegal_csr;\n\n  always_comb begin : csr_read\n    illegal_csr   = 1'b0;\n    csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n\n    unique case (csr_addr_i)\n      CSR_MHARTID:   csr_rdata_int = hart_id_i;\n      CSR_MVENDORID: csr_rdata_int = CsrMvendorId;\n      CSR_MARCHID:   csr_rdata_int = 32'd22; // Ibex\n      CSR_MIMPID:    csr_rdata_int = CsrMimpId;\n      CSR_MCONFIGPTR: csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n\n      // mstatus minimal\n      CSR_MSTATUS:   csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n      CSR_MSTATUSH:  csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n      CSR_MISA:      csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n      CSR_MIE:       csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n      CSR_MIP:       csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n      CSR_MTVEC:     csr_rdata_int = mtvec_q;\n      CSR_MSCRATCH:  csr_rdata_int = mscratch_q;\n      CSR_MEPC:      csr_rdata_int = mepc_q;\n      CSR_MCAUSE:    csr_rdata_int = {mcause_q.irq_ext | mcause_q.irq_int, 26'b0,\n                                       mcause_q.irq_int ? 5'd31 : mcause_q.lower_cause};\n      CSR_MTVAL:     csr_rdata_int = mtval_q;\n      CSR_MCOUNTEREN: csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n\n      // PMP cfg\n      CSR_PMPCFG0,\n      CSR_PMPCFG1,\n      CSR_PMPCFG2,\n      CSR_PMPCFG3: begin\n        // Determine which 4 regions\n        logic [1:0] idx;\n        logic [31:0] val;\n        idx = csr_addr_i[1:0];\n        val = 32'h0;\n        for (int unsigned i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin\n          int unsigned reg_idx;\n          reg_idx = idx * 4 + i;\n          if (PMPEnable && (reg_idx < PMPNumRegions)) begin\n            val[i*8 +: 8] = pack_pmp_cfg(pmp_cfg_q[reg_idx]);\n          end\n        end\n        csr_rdata_int = val;\n      end\n\n      // PMP addr (PMPAddr0-15)\n      CSR_PMPADDR0, CSR_PMPADDR1, CSR_PMPADDR2, CSR_PMPADDR3,\n      CSR_PMPADDR4, CSR_PMPADDR5, CSR_PMPADDR6, CSR_PMPADDR7,\n      CSR_PMPADDR8, CSR_PMPADDR9, CSR_PMPADDR10, CSR_PMPADDR11,\n      CSR_PMPADDR12, CSR_PMPADDR13, CSR_PMPADDR14, CSR_PMPADDR15: begin\n        int unsigned ridx;\n        ridx = csr_addr_i[3:0];\n        if (PMPEnable && (ridx < PMPNumRegions)) begin\n          csr_rdata_int = pmp_addr_q[ridx][33:2];\n        end else begin\n          csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        end\n      end\n\n      CSR_MSECCFG: begin\n        csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        csr_rdata_int[0] = pmp_mseccfg_q.mml;\n        csr_rdata_int[1] = pmp_mseccfg_q.mmwp;\n        csr_rdata_int[2] = pmp_mseccfg_q.rlb;\n      end\n\n      CSR_MSECCFGH: csr_rdata_int = 32'h0; // not implemented\n\n      // mcountinhibit\n      CSR_MCOUNTINHIBIT: csr_rdata_int = mcountinhibit_q;\n\n      // mhpmevent3..31\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT3, CSR_MHPMEVENT4, CSR_MHPMEVENT5, CSR_MHPMEVENT6,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT7, CSR_MHPMEVENT8, CSR_MHPMEVENT9, CSR_MHPMEVENT10,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT11, CSR_MHPMEVENT12, CSR_MHPMEVENT13, CSR_MHPMEVENT14,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT15, CSR_MHPMEVENT16, CSR_MHPMEVENT17, CSR_MHPMEVENT18,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT19, CSR_MHPMEVENT20, CSR_MHPMEVENT21, CSR_MHPMEVENT22,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT23, CSR_MHPMEVENT24, CSR_MHPMEVENT25, CSR_MHPMEVENT26,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT27, CSR_MHPMEVENT28, CSR_MHPMEVENT29, CSR_MHPMEVENT30,\n      CSR_MHPMEVENT31: begin\n        int unsigned cidx;\n        cidx = csr_addr_i[4:0]; // 0x320..0x33F: low 5 bits = index\n        if ((cidx >= 3) && (cidx < (MHPMCounterNum + 3))) begin\n          csr_rdata_int = mhpmevent_q[cidx];\n        end else begin\n          csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        end\n      end\n\n      CSR_MCYCLE:   csr_rdata_int = mcycle_q[31:0];\n      CSR_MCYCLEH:  csr_rdata_int = mcycle_q[63:32];\n      CSR_MINSTRET: csr_rdata_int = minstret_q[31:0];\n      CSR_MINSTRETH: csr_rdata_int = minstret_q[63:32];\n\n      // mhpmcounter low (B03..B1F)\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER3, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER4, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER5, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER6,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER7, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER8, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER9, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER10,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER11, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER12, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER13, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER14,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER15, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER16, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER17, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER18,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER19, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER20, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER21, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER22,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER23, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER24, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER25, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER26,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER27, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER28, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER29, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER30,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER31: begin\n        int unsigned cidx;\n        cidx = csr_addr_i[4:0];\n        if ((cidx >= 3) && (cidx < (MHPMCounterNum + 3))) begin\n          csr_rdata_int = mhpmcounter_q[cidx][31:0];\n        end else begin\n          csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        end\n      end\n\n      // mhpmcounter high (B83..B9F)\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER3H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER4H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER5H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER6H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER7H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER8H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER9H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER10H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER11H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER12H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER13H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER14H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER15H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER16H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER17H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER18H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER19H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER20H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER21H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER22H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER23H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER24H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER25H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER26H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER27H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER28H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER29H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER30H,\n      CSR_MHPMCOUNTER31H: begin\n        int unsigned cidx;\n        cidx = csr_addr_i[4:0];\n        if ((cidx >= 3) && (cidx < (MHPMCounterNum + 3))) begin\n          csr_rdata_int = mhpmcounter_q[cidx][63:32];\n        end else begin\n          csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        end\n      end\n\n      default: begin\n        csr_rdata_int = 32'h0;\n        // illegal_csr = 1'b1; // permissive: don't flag in test\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign csr_rdata_o = csr_rdata_int;\n  assign illegal_csr_insn_o = illegal_csr & csr_access_i;\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // CSR write logic\n  // ====================================================\n\n  // Determine MSeccfg next value with all locking semantics\n  function automatic pmp_mseccfg_t mseccfg_next(pmp_mseccfg_t cur, logic [31:0] wd);\n    pmp_mseccfg_t nxt;\n    logic         any_pmp_locked;\n    logic         allow_rlb;\n    any_pmp_locked = 1'b0;\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < PMPNumRegions; i++) begin\n      if (pmp_cfg_q[i].lock) begin\n        any_pmp_locked = 1'b1;\n      end\n    end\n    // Default: bottom 3 bits are settable subject to lock rules\n    nxt = cur;\n    // Bit 2 (RLB): if currently 0 and any pmp locked, RLB stays 0\n    if (cur.rlb || !any_pmp_locked) begin\n      nxt.rlb = wd[2];\n    end\n    // Bit 1 (MMWP): once set, stays set\n    if (cur.mmwp) begin\n      nxt.mmwp = 1'b1;\n    end else begin\n      nxt.mmwp = wd[1];\n    end\n    // Bit 0 (MML): once set, stays set\n    if (cur.mml) begin\n      nxt.mml = 1'b1;\n    end else begin\n      nxt.mml = wd[0];\n    end\n    return nxt;\n  endfunction\n\n  // Determine if a PMPAddr region can be written, per the model's lock rule:\n  // locked if own.L == 1 OR next region's mode == TOR.\n  function automatic logic pmp_addr_locked(int unsigned r);\n    logic locked_self;\n    logic next_tor;\n    locked_self = 1'b0;\n    next_tor    = 1'b0;\n    if (r < PMPNumRegions) begin\n      locked_self = pmp_cfg_q[r].lock;\n    end\n    if ((r + 1) < PMPNumRegions) begin\n      next_tor = (pmp_cfg_q[r+1].mode == PMP_MODE_TOR);\n    end\n    return locked_self | next_tor;\n  endfunction\n\n  // Compute write masks for mhpmcounter based on MHPMCounterWidth\n  // mask bits set = locked (read as zero)\n  localparam logic [63:0] MhpmcMaskCalc = (MHPMCounterWidth >= 64) ?\n                                          64'h0 :\n                                          (~((64'h1 << MHPMCounterWidth) - 64'h1));\n  localparam logic [31:0] MhpmcMaskLo = MhpmcMaskCalc[31:0];\n  localparam logic [31:0] MhpmcMaskHi = MhpmcMaskCalc[63:32];\n\n  // mcountinhibit mask (bits set = locked)\n  localparam logic [31:0] McountInhibitMask =\n    ((MHPMCounterNum >= 32) ? 32'h0 :\n     ((~((32'h1 << MHPMCounterNum) - 32'h1)) << 3)) | 32'h2;\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // CSR write update (sequential)\n  // ====================================================\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk_i or negedge rst_ni) begin : csr_update\n    if (!rst_ni) begin\n      // Reset\n      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < PMP_MAX_REGIONS; i++) begin\n        pmp_cfg_q[i]  <= PMPRstCfg[i];\n        pmp_addr_q[i] <= PMPRstAddr[i];\n      end\n      pmp_mseccfg_q   <= PMPRstMsecCfg;\n      mcountinhibit_q <= 32'h0;\n      for (int unsigned i = 3; i < 32; i++) begin\n        mhpmevent_q[i] <= 32'h1 << (i - 3);\n      end\n      mcycle_q   <= 64'h0;\n      minstret_q <= 64'h0;\n      for (int unsigned i = 3; i < 32; i++) begin\n        mhpmcounter_q[i] <= 64'h0;\n      end\n      mtvec_q   <= 32'h0;\n      mepc_q    <= 32'h0;\n      mcause_q  <= '{irq_ext: 1'b0, irq_int: 1'b0, lower_cause: 5'h0};\n      mtval_q   <= 32'h0;\n      mscratch_q<= 32'h0;\n    end else begin\n      // mcycle increments every cycle unless inhibited\n      if (!mcountinhibit_q[0]) begin\n        mcycle_q <= mcycle_q + 64'h1;\n      end\n\n      // Default: minstret/mhpmcounter no auto-increment in tb_cs_registers\n      // (would normally depend on event signals)\n\n      if (csr_op_en_i) begin\n        unique case (csr_addr_i)\n          CSR_MTVEC:    mtvec_q    <= csr_wdata_int;\n          CSR_MSCRATCH: mscratch_q <= csr_wdata_int;\n          CSR_MEPC:     mepc_q     <= {csr_wdata_int[31:2], 2'b00};\n          CSR_MCAUSE: begin\n            mcause_q.irq_ext     <= csr_wdata_int[31];\n            mcause_q.irq_int     <= 1'b0;\n            mcause_q.lower_cause <= csr_wdata_int[4:0];\n          end\n          CSR_MTVAL:    mtval_q    <= csr_wdata_int;\n\n          // PMP CFG (4 bytes per write)\n          CSR_PMPCFG0, CSR_PMPCFG1, CSR_PMPCFG2, CSR_PMPCFG3: begin\n            logic [1:0] idx;\n            idx = csr_addr_i[1:0];\n            if (PMPEnable) begin\n              for (int unsigned i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin\n                int unsigned r;\n                logic [7:0]  cur_b;\n                logic [7:0]  nxt_b;\n                logic [7:0]  fin_b;\n                r = idx * 4 + i;\n                if (r < PMPNumRegions) begin\n                  cur_b = pack_pmp_cfg(pmp_cfg_q[r]);\n                  nxt_b = csr_wdata_int[i*8 +: 8];\n                  fin_b = apply_pmp_cfg_byte_write(cur_b, nxt_b, pmp_mseccfg_q);\n                  pmp_cfg_q[r] <= unpack_pmp_cfg(fin_b);\n                end\n              end\n            end\n          end\n\n          // PMP ADDR\n          CSR_PMPADDR0, CSR_PMPADDR1, CSR_PMPADDR2, CSR_PMPADDR3,\n          CSR_PMPADDR4, CSR_PMPADDR5, CSR_PMPADDR6, CSR_PMPADDR7,\n          CSR_PMPADDR8, CSR_PMPADDR9, CSR_PMPADDR10, CSR_PMPADDR11,\n          CSR_PMPADDR12, CSR_PMPADDR13, CSR_PMPADDR14, CSR_PMPADDR15: begin\n            int unsigned ridx;\n            ridx = csr_addr_i[3:0];\n            if (PMPEnable && (ridx < PMPNumRegions)) begin\n              if (!pmp_addr_locked(ridx)) begin\n                pmp_addr_q[ridx] <= {csr_wdata_int, 2'b00};\n              end\n            end\n          end\n\n          CSR_MSECCFG: begin\n            pmp_mseccfg_q <= mseccfg_next(pmp_mseccfg_q, csr_wdata_int);\n          end\n\n          CSR_MSECCFGH: begin\n            // not implemented; ignore writes\n          end\n\n          CSR_MCOUNTINHIBIT: begin\n            mcountinhibit_q <= csr_wdata_int & ~McountInhibitMask;\n          end\n\n          // mhpmevent: WARL-RO (mask = 0xFFFFFFFF locks all bits) - ignore writes\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT3, CSR_MHPMEVENT4, CSR_MHPMEVENT5, CSR_MHPMEVENT6,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT7, CSR_MHPMEVENT8, CSR_MHPMEVENT9, CSR_MHPMEVENT10,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT11, CSR_MHPMEVENT12, CSR_MHPMEVENT13, CSR_MHPMEVENT14,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT15, CSR_MHPMEVENT16, CSR_MHPMEVENT17, CSR_MHPMEVENT18,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT19, CSR_MHPMEVENT20, CSR_MHPMEVENT21, CSR_MHPMEVENT22,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT23, CSR_MHPMEVENT24, CSR_MHPMEVENT25, CSR_MHPMEVENT26,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT27, CSR_MHPMEVENT28, CSR_MHPMEVENT29, CSR_MHPMEVENT30,\n          CSR_MHPMEVENT31: begin\n            // read-only; ignore writes\n          end\n\n          CSR_MCYCLE:   mcycle_q[31:0]   <= csr_wdata_int;\n          CSR_MCYCLEH:  mcycle_q[63:32]  <= csr_wdata_int;\n          CSR_MINSTRET: minstret_q[31:0] <= csr_wdata_int;\n          CSR_MINSTRETH: minstret_q[63:32] <= csr_wdata_int;\n\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER3, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER4, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER5, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER6,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER7, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER8, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER9, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER10,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER11, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER12, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER13, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER14,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER15, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER16, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER17, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER18,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER19, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER20, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER21, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER22,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER23, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER24, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER25, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER26,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER27, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER28, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER29, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER30,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER31: begin\n            int unsigned cidx;\n            cidx = csr_addr_i[4:0];\n            if ((cidx >= 3) && (cidx < (MHPMCounterNum + 3))) begin\n              mhpmcounter_q[cidx][31:0] <= csr_wdata_int & ~MhpmcMaskLo;\n            end\n          end\n\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER3H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER4H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER5H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER6H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER7H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER8H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER9H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER10H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER11H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER12H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER13H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER14H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER15H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER16H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER17H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER18H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER19H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER20H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER21H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER22H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER23H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER24H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER25H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER26H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER27H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER28H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER29H, CSR_MHPMCOUNTER30H,\n          CSR_MHPMCOUNTER31H: begin\n            int unsigned cidx;\n            cidx = csr_addr_i[4:0];\n            if ((cidx >= 3) && (cidx < (MHPMCounterNum + 3))) begin\n              mhpmcounter_q[cidx][63:32] <= csr_wdata_int & ~MhpmcMaskHi;\n            end\n          end\n\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ====================================================\n  // Outputs\n  // ====================================================\n\n  // PMP outputs\n  for (genvar i = 0; i < PMPNumRegions; i++) begin : g_pmp_outs\n    assign csr_pmp_cfg_o[i]  = pmp_cfg_q[i];\n    assign csr_pmp_addr_o[i] = pmp_addr_q[i];\n  end\n  assign csr_pmp_mseccfg_o = pmp_mseccfg_q;\n\n  // Default outputs (not exercised by tb_cs_registers)\n  assign priv_mode_id_o        = PRIV_LVL_M;\n  assign priv_mode_lsu_o       = PRIV_LVL_M;\n  assign csr_mtvec_o           = mtvec_q;\n  assign irq_pending_o         = 1'b0;\n  assign irqs_o                = '0;\n  assign csr_mstatus_mie_o     = 1'b0;\n  assign csr_mstatus_tw_o      = 1'b0;\n  assign csr_mepc_o            = mepc_q;\n  assign csr_mtval_o           = mtval_q;\n  assign csr_depc_o            = '0;\n  assign debug_single_step_o   = 1'b0;\n  assign debug_ebreakm_o       = 1'b0;\n  assign debug_ebreaku_o       = 1'b0;\n  assign trigger_match_o       = 1'b0;\n  assign data_ind_timing_o     = 1'b0;\n  assign dummy_instr_en_o      = 1'b0;\n  assign dummy_instr_mask_o    = 3'b000;\n  assign dummy_instr_seed_en_o = 1'b0;\n  assign dummy_instr_seed_o    = 32'h0;\n  assign icache_enable_o       = 1'b0;\n  assign csr_shadow_err_o      = 1'b0;\n  assign double_fault_seen_o   = 1'b0;\n\n  // Tie off unused inputs to silence lint\n  logic                  unused_csr_mtvec_init;\n  logic [31:0]           unused_boot_addr;\n  logic                  unused_irq_software, unused_irq_timer, unused_irq_external;\n  logic [14:0]           unused_irq_fast;\n  logic                  unused_nmi_mode;\n  logic                  unused_debug_mode, unused_debug_mode_entering;\n  dbg_cause_e            unused_debug_cause;\n  logic                  unused_debug_csr_save;\n  logic [31:0]           unused_pc_if, unused_pc_id, unused_pc_wb;\n  logic                  unused_ic_scr_key_valid;\n  logic                  unused_csr_save_if, unused_csr_save_id, unused_csr_save_wb;\n  logic                  unused_csr_restore_mret, unused_csr_restore_dret;\n  logic                  unused_csr_save_cause;\n  exc_cause_t            unused_csr_mcause;\n  logic [31:0]           unused_csr_mtval_in;\n  logic                  unused_instr_ret, unused_instr_ret_compressed;\n  logic                  unused_instr_ret_spec, unused_instr_ret_compressed_spec;\n  logic                  unused_iside_wait, unused_jump, unused_branch;\n  logic                  unused_branch_taken, unused_mem_load, unused_mem_store;\n  logic                  unused_dside_wait, unused_mul_wait, unused_div_wait;\n\n  assign unused_csr_mtvec_init      = csr_mtvec_init_i;\n  assign unused_boot_addr           = boot_addr_i;\n  assign unused_irq_software        = irq_software_i;\n  assign unused_irq_timer           = irq_timer_i;\n  assign unused_irq_external        = irq_external_i;\n  assign unused_irq_fast            = irq_fast_i;\n  assign unused_nmi_mode            = nmi_mode_i;\n  assign unused_debug_mode          = debug_mode_i;\n  assign unused_debug_mode_entering = function automatic pmp_mseccfg_t mseccfg_next(pmp_mseccfg_t cur, logic [31:0] wd);\n    pmp_mseccfg_t nxt;\n    logic         any_pmp_locked;\n    logic         allow_rlb;\n    any_pmp_locked = 1'b0;\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < PMPNumRegions; i++) begin\n      if (pmp_cfg_q[i].lock) begin\n        any_pmp_locked = 1'b1;\n      end\n    end\n    // Default: bottom 3 bits are settable subject to lock rules\n    nxt = cur;\n    // Bit 2 (RLB): if currently 0 and any pmp locked, RLB stays 0\n    if (cur.rlb || !any_pmp_locked) begin\n      nxt.rlb = wd[2];\n    end\n    // Bit 1 (MMWP): once set, stays set\n    if (cur.mmwp) begin\n      nxt.mmwp = 1'b1;\n    end else begin\n      nxt.mmwp = wd[1];\n    end\n    // Bit 0 (MML): once set, stays set\n    if (cur.mml) begin\n      nxt.mml = 1'b1;\n    end else begin\n      nxt.mml = wd[0];\n    end\n    return nxt;\n  endfunction\n\n  // Determine if a PMPAddr region can be written, per the model's lock rule:\n  // locked if own.L == 1 OR next region's mode == TOR.\n  function automatic logic pmp_addr_locked(int unsigned r);\n    logic locked_self;\n    logic next_tor;\n    locked_self = 1'b0;\n    next_tor    = 1'b0;\n    if (r < PMPNumRegions) begin\n      locked_self = pmp_cfg_q[r].lock;\n    end\n    if ((r + 1) < PMPNumRegions) begin\n      next_tor = (pmp_cfg_q[r+1].mode == PMP_MODE_TOR);\n    end\n    return locked_self | next_tor;\n  endfunction\n\n  // Compute write masks for mhpmcounter based on MHPMCounterWidth\n  // mask bits set = locked (read as zero)\n  localparam logic [63:0] MhpmcMaskCalc = (MHPMCounterWidth >= 64) ?\n                                          64'h0 :\n                                          (~((64'h1 << MHPMCounterWidth) - 64'h1));\n  localparam logic [31:0]
